2098 | // -----------------------------------------------------------------
2099 | // WiiMix Determinism Diff Test
2100 | // -----------------------------------------------------------------
2101 | // This is your primary debugging tool.
2102 | // It proves that your save/load functions are working correctly.
2103 | // 1. Saves a "portable" state to buffer (A)
2104 | // 2. Steps the emulator one frame, saves the result (B)
2105 | // 3. Loads state (A) back (which must trigger host re-init)
2106 | // 4. Steps the emulator one frame, saves the result (C)
2107 | // 5. Compares B and C. If they match, your functions are deterministic.
2108 | 
2109 | // ORIGINAL
2110 | // void WiiMixStepNPauses(Core::System& system, int n)
2111 | // {
2112 | //   for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
2113 | //   {
2114 | //     // 1. Trigger the internal frame step logic.
2115 | //     // This sets s_frame_step = true and unpauses the system.
2116 | //     Core::DoFrameStep(system);
2117 | 
2118 | //     // 2. FORCE the "Frame Presented" signal.
2119 | //     // In Headless/NoGUI mode, the video backend often skips presentation or
2120 | //     // doesn't trigger the callback. This manually sets s_stop_frame_step = true.
2121 | //     // We do this immediately. The Emulator thread (Interpreter) is slow enough
2122 | //     // that this flag will definitely be set before the CPU reaches the next
2123 | //     // VICallback (Field boundary), ensuring we stop at exactly the next Field.
2124 | //     Core::Callback_FramePresented(1.0);
2125 | 
2126 | //     // 3. Wait for the EmuThread to pause itself at the VI boundary.
2127 | //     // We use a small sleep to yield the Host thread while waiting.
2128 | //     // We also add a timeout just in case.
2129 | //     int timeout_ms = 5000;
2130 | //     while (Core::GetState(system) != Core::State::Paused)
2131 | //     {
2132 | //       std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(1));
2133 | //       timeout_ms--;
2134 | //       if (timeout_ms <= 0) {
2135 | //           printf("WII-MIX-ERROR: Timed out waiting for frame step to finish!\n");
2136 | //           // Force break to unstick the thread, though the test will likely fail
2137 | //           system.GetCPU().Break();
2138 | //           break;
2139 | //       }
2140 | //     }
2141 | //   }
2142 | // }
2143 | 
2144 | void WiiMixStepNPauses(Core::System& system, int n)
2145 | {
2146 |   for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
2147 |   {
2148 |     // 1. Trigger the internal frame step logic.
2149 |     Core::DoFrameStep(system);
2150 | 
2151 |     // 2. FORCE the "Frame Presented" signal immediately.
2152 |     // In headless mode, we cannot rely on the GPU thread to swap buffers correctly
2153 |     // after a poison event. We emulate the signal so the CPU thread stops at the VI.
2154 |     Core::Callback_FramePresented(1.0);
2155 | 
2156 |     // 3. Wait for the EmuThread to pause itself.
2157 |     // Use a timeout to prevent deadlocks.
2158 |     int timeout_ms = 2000;
2159 |     while (Core::GetState(system) != Core::State::Paused)
2160 |     {
2161 |       std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(1));
2162 |       timeout_ms--;
2163 |       if (timeout_ms <= 0) {
2164 |           printf("WII-MIX-ERROR: Timed out waiting for frame step to finish! Forcing Break.\n");
2165 |           system.GetCPU().Break(); // Force CPU loop to exit
2166 | 
2167 |           // Wait a bit for the thread to actually stop
2168 |           std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(100));
2169 |           return;
2170 |       }
2171 |     }
2172 |   }
2173 | }
2174 | 
2175 | int WiiMixIdentityTest(Core::System& system)
2176 | {
2177 |   printf("Starting WiiMix Identity Test...\n");
2178 | 
2179 |   // 1. Warm-up Phase
2180 |   // Run 10 frames to ensure the emulator is in a steady state (past boot ROM, etc).
2181 |   // Initial boot state often has unique flags that are cleared after first use.
2182 |   Core::SetState(system, Core::State::Paused, false);
2183 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&]() {}, true); // Sync
2184 | 
2185 |   printf("Warming up (10 frames)...\n");
2186 |   WiiMixStepNPauses(system, 10);
2187 | 
2188 |   int exit_code = 0;
2189 |   std::vector<u8> buffer_A;
2190 |   std::vector<u8> buffer_B;
2191 | 
2192 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&] {
2193 |     // 2. Save State A
2194 |     printf("Saving State A...\n");
2195 |     WiiMixSaveToBuffer(system, buffer_A);
2196 |     if (buffer_A.empty()) {
2197 |         printf("IDENTITY FAIL: Buffer A is empty.\n");
2198 |         exit_code = 2;
2199 |         return;
2200 |     }
2201 | 
2202 |     // 3. Load State A
2203 |     // Note: WiiMixLoadFromBufferEmuThread handles Poisoning internally if configured via CLI
2204 |     printf("Loading State A (Triggering Re-Init/Poison)...\n");
2205 |     bool load_ok = WiiMixLoadFromBufferEmuThread(system, buffer_A);
2206 |     if (!load_ok) {
2207 |         printf("IDENTITY FAIL: Load returned false.\n");
2208 |         exit_code = 3;
2209 |         return;
2210 |     }
2211 | 
2212 |     // 4. Save State B (Immediately after load)
2213 |     // Theoretically, State B should be bit-for-bit identical to State A
2214 |     printf("Saving State B...\n");
2215 |     WiiMixSaveToBuffer(system, buffer_B);
2216 | 
2217 |     // 5. Compare
2218 |     if (buffer_A.size() != buffer_B.size()) {
2219 |         printf("IDENTITY FAIL: Size mismatch. A: %zu, B: %zu\n", buffer_A.size(), buffer_B.size());
2220 |         exit_code = 10;
2221 |     }
2222 |     else if (buffer_A != buffer_B) {
2223 |         // Find the first mismatch offset for debugging
2224 |         size_t mismatch_idx = 0;
2225 |         for (; mismatch_idx < buffer_A.size(); ++mismatch_idx) {
2226 |             if (buffer_A[mismatch_idx] != buffer_B[mismatch_idx]) break;
2227 |         }
2228 |         printf("IDENTITY FAIL: Content mismatch at offset 0x%zX\n", mismatch_idx);
2229 |         exit_code = 10;
2230 |     }
2231 |     else {
2232 |         printf("Identity Test PASSED! Load(Save(S)) == S\n");
2233 |         exit_code = 0;
2234 |     }
2235 | 
2236 |     // Dump files if failed
2237 |     if (exit_code != 0) {
2238 |         File::IOFile file_a("Identity_A.bin", "wb");
2239 |         if(file_a) file_a.WriteBytes(buffer_A.data(), buffer_A.size());
2240 | 
2241 |         File::IOFile file_b("Identity_B.bin", "wb");
2242 |         if(file_b) file_b.WriteBytes(buffer_B.data(), buffer_B.size());
2243 |         printf("Dumped Identity_A.bin and Identity_B.bin for analysis.\n");
2244 |     }
2245 | 
2246 |     Core::Stop(system);
2247 |   }, true);
2248 | 
2249 |   return exit_code;
2250 | }
2251 | 
2252 | int WiiMixDiffTest(Core::System& system, int steps)
2253 | {
2254 |   printf("Starting WiiMix Diff Test (%d frames)...\n", steps);
2255 |   int exit_code = 1;
2256 |   std::vector<u8> buffer_A, buffer_B, buffer_C;
2257 |   bool emu_thread_ok = true;
2258 | 
2259 |   // Pause the EmuThread
2260 |   Core::SetState(system, Core::State::Paused, false);
2261 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&]() {}, true);
2262 | 
2263 |   // --- Save A (The "Anchor" State) ---
2264 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&] {
2265 |     printf("WII-MIX-TEST-LOG: Save A START (Frame: %ld)\n", system.GetMovie().GetCurrentFrame());
2266 |     WiiMixSaveToBuffer(system, buffer_A);
2267 |     if (buffer_A.empty()) { emu_thread_ok = false; return; }
2268 |   }, true);
2269 | 
2270 |   if (!emu_thread_ok) {
2271 |     TEST_FAIL(2, "Diff Test FAILED: State A was empty.");
2272 |     return 2;
2273 |   }
2274 | 
2275 |   // --- Step N Frames (Path 1) ---
2276 |   printf("Stepping %d frames (Continuous)...\n", steps);
2277 | 
2278 |   // NOTE: Tracing disabled for speed. Uncomment only for deep debugging single frames.
2279 |   // Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&] { GetInterpreter(system)->StartPCTrace("trace_B.txt"); }, true);
2280 |   WiiMixStepNPauses(system, steps);
2281 |   // Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&] { GetInterpreter(system)->StopPCTrace(); }, true);
2282 | 
2283 |   // --- Save B (Continuous Result) ---
2284 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&] {
2285 |     printf("WII-MIX-TEST-LOG: Save B START (Frame: %ld)\n", system.GetMovie().GetCurrentFrame());
2286 |     WiiMixSaveToBuffer(system, buffer_B);
2287 |     if (buffer_B.empty()) { emu_thread_ok = false; return; }
2288 |   }, true);
2289 | 
2290 |   if (!emu_thread_ok) {
2291 |     TEST_FAIL(4, "Diff Test FAILED: State B was empty.");
2292 |     return 4;
2293 |   }
2294 | 
2295 |   // --- Load A (Reset) ---
2296 |   printf("Loading State A...\n");
2297 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&] {
2298 |     // Poisoning is handled inside WiiMixLoadFromBufferEmuThread based on Config::WIIMIX_POISON_MASK
2299 |     if (!WiiMixLoadFromBufferEmuThread(system, buffer_A)) {
2300 |       emu_thread_ok = false;
2301 |     }
2302 |   }, true);
2303 | 
2304 |   if (!emu_thread_ok) {
2305 |     TEST_FAIL(6, "Diff Test FAILED: Loading Emulated State failed.");
2306 |     return 6;
2307 |   }
2308 | 
2309 |   // --- Step N Frames (Path 2) ---
2310 |   printf("Stepping %d frames (Loaded)...\n", steps);
2311 | 
2312 |   // NOTE: Tracing disabled for speed.
2313 |   // Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&] { GetInterpreter(system)->StartPCTrace("trace_C.txt"); }, true);
2314 |   WiiMixStepNPauses(system, steps);
2315 |   // Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&] { GetInterpreter(system)->StopPCTrace(); }, true);
2316 | 
2317 |   // --- Save C (Loaded Result) ---
2318 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(system, [&] {
2319 |     printf("WII-MIX-TEST-LOG: Save C START (Frame: %ld)\n", system.GetMovie().GetCurrentFrame());
2320 |     WiiMixSaveToBuffer(system, buffer_C);
2321 | 
2322 |     if (buffer_C.empty()) {
2323 |       TEST_FAIL(8, "Diff Test FAILED: State C was empty.");
2324 |       exit_code = 8;
2325 |     } else if (buffer_B.size() != buffer_C.size()) {
2326 |       TEST_FAIL(9, "Diff Test FAILED: Size mismatch! B=%zu, C=%zu.", buffer_B.size(), buffer_C.size());
2327 |       exit_code = 9;
2328 |     } else if (buffer_B == buffer_C) {
2329 |       printf("Diff Test PASSED! State is deterministic.\n");
2330 |       exit_code = 0;
2331 |     } else {
2332 |       auto mismatch = std::mismatch(buffer_B.begin(), buffer_B.end(), buffer_C.begin()).first;
2333 |       size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(mismatch - buffer_B.begin());
2334 |       TEST_FAIL(10, "Diff Test FAILED: Mismatch at byte 0x%zX Your save/load logic is not deterministic.", offset);
2335 |       exit_code = 10;
2336 |     }
2337 | 
2338 |     if (exit_code != 0) {
2339 |       File::IOFile file_b("B.bin", "wb");
2340 |       if (file_b) file_b.WriteBytes(buffer_B.data(), buffer_B.size());
2341 |       File::IOFile file_c("C.bin", "wb");
2342 |       if (file_c) file_c.WriteBytes(buffer_C.data(), buffer_C.size());
2343 |     }
2344 | 
2345 |     Core::Stop(system);
2346 |   }, true);
2347 | 
2348 |   return exit_code;
2349 | }
2350 | }  // namespace State

 152 | static std::condition_variable s_state_write_queue_is_empty;
 153 | 
 154 | // Don't forget to increase this after doing changes on the savestate system
 155 | constexpr u32 STATE_VERSION = 168;  // Last changed in PR 12639

 157 | // Increase this if the StateExtendedHeader definition changes
 158 | constexpr u32 EXTENDED_HEADER_VERSION = 1;  // Last changed in PR 12217
 159 | 
 160 | // Change this if we ever need to store more data in the extended header
 161 | constexpr u32 COMPRESSED_DATA_OFFSET = 0;
 162 | 
 163 | constexpr u32 COOKIE_BASE = 0xBAADBABE;

 167 | // because they save the exact Dolphin version to savestates.
 168 | static const std::map<u32, std::pair<std::string, std::string>> s_old_versions = {
 169 |     // The 16 -> 17 change modified the size of StateHeader,
 170 |     // so versions older than that can't even be decompressed anymore

 172 |     {20, {"3.5-1409", "4.0-704"}},  {21, {"4.0-705", "4.0-889"}},   {22, {"4.0-905", "4.0-1871"}},
 173 |     {23, {"4.0-1873", "4.0-1900"}}, {24, {"4.0-1902", "4.0-1919"}}, {25, {"4.0-1921", "4.0-1936"}},

 181 | enum
 182 | {
 183 |   STATE_NONE = 0,
 184 |   STATE_SAVE = 1,
 185 |   STATE_LOAD = 2,

 187 | 
 188 | static bool s_use_compression = true;
 189 | 
 190 | Interpreter* GetInterpreter(Core::System& system)
 191 | {
 192 |   // 1. Get the JitInterface& manager from the system
 193 |   JitInterface& jit_manager = system.GetJitInterface();
 194 | 
 195 |   // 2. Get the generic CPUCoreBase* pointer from the manager
 196 |   CPUCoreBase* cpu_core = jit_manager.GetCore();
 197 | 
 198 |   // 3. Now, we can safely dynamic_cast the CPUCoreBase* to an Interpreter*
 199 |   return dynamic_cast<Interpreter*>(cpu_core);
 200 | }
 201 | 
 202 | void EnableCompression(bool compression)
 203 | {
 204 |   s_use_compression = compression;
 205 | }
 206 | 
 207 | static void DoState(Core::System& system, PointerWrap& p)
 208 | {
 209 |   bool is_wii = system.IsWii() || system.IsMIOS();
 210 |   const bool is_wii_currently = is_wii;
 211 |   p.Do(is_wii);
 212 |   if (is_wii != is_wii_currently)
 213 |   {
 214 |     OSD::AddMessage(fmt::format("Cannot load a savestate created under {} mode in {} mode",
 215 |                                 is_wii ? "Wii" : "GC", is_wii_currently ? "Wii" : "GC"),
 216 |                     OSD::Duration::NORMAL, OSD::Color::RED);
 217 |     p.SetMeasureMode();
 218 |     return;
 219 |   }
 220 | 
 221 |   // Check to make sure the emulated memory sizes are the same as the savestate
 222 |   auto& memory = system.GetMemory();
 223 |   u32 state_mem1_size = memory.GetRamSizeReal();
 224 |   u32 state_mem2_size = memory.GetExRamSizeReal();
 225 |   p.Do(state_mem1_size);
 226 |   p.Do(state_mem2_size);
 227 |   if (state_mem1_size != memory.GetRamSizeReal() || state_mem2_size != memory.GetExRamSizeReal())
 228 |   {
 229 |     OSD::AddMessage(fmt::format("Memory size mismatch!\n"
 230 |                                 "Current | MEM1 {:08X} ({:3}MB)    MEM2 {:08X} ({:3}MB)\n"
 231 |                                 "State   | MEM1 {:08X} ({:3}MB)    MEM2 {:08X} ({:3}MB)",
 232 |                                 memory.GetRamSizeReal(), memory.GetRamSizeReal() / 0x100000U,
 233 |                                 memory.GetExRamSizeReal(), memory.GetExRamSizeReal() / 0x100000U,
 234 |                                 state_mem1_size, state_mem1_size / 0x100000U, state_mem2_size,
 235 |                                 state_mem2_size / 0x100000U));
 236 |     p.SetMeasureMode();
 237 |     return;
 238 |   }
 239 | 
 240 |   // Movie must be done before the video backend, because the window is redrawn in the video backend
 241 |   // state load, and the frame number must be up-to-date.
 242 |   system.GetMovie().DoState(p);
 243 |   p.DoMarker("Movie");
 244 |   // CoreTiming needs to be restored before restoring Hardware because
 245 |   // the controller code might need to schedule an event if the controller has changed.
 246 |   system.GetCoreTiming().DoState(p);
 247 |   p.DoMarker("CoreTiming");
 248 | 
 249 |   // HW needs to be restored before PowerPC because the data cache might need to be flushed.
 250 |   HW::DoState(system, p);
 251 |   p.DoMarker("HW");
 252 | 
 253 |   g_video_backend->DoState(p);
 254 |   p.DoMarker("video_backend");
 255 | 
 256 |   system.GetPowerPC().DoState(p);
 257 |   p.DoMarker("PowerPC");
 258 | 
 259 |   if (system.IsWii())
 260 |     Wiimote::DoState(p);
 261 |   p.DoMarker("Wiimote");
 262 |   Gecko::DoState(p);
 263 |   p.DoMarker("Gecko");
 264 | 
 265 |   #ifdef USE_RETRO_ACHIEVEMENTS
 266 |     AchievementManager::GetInstance().DoState(p);
 267 |   #endif  // USE_RETRO_ACHIEVEMENTS
 268 | }
 269 | 
 270 | void LogOffset(const char* name, PointerWrap& p)
 271 | {
 272 |   if (p.IsMeasureMode()) {
 273 |       // In Measure mode, ptr *is* the offset
 274 |       printf("WII-MIX-MAP: Offset after %s: 0x%zX\n", name, reinterpret_cast<size_t>(p.GetCurrentPtr()));
 275 |   }
 276 | }
 277 | 
 278 | void LogMap(PointerWrap& p, const char* name) {
 279 |     if (p.IsMeasureMode()) {
 280 |         size_t offset = reinterpret_cast<size_t>(p.GetCurrentPtr());
 281 |         // Format: [MAP],OffsetDec,OffsetHex,VariableName
 282 |         printf("[MAP],%zu,%zX,%s\n", offset, offset, name);
 283 |     }
 284 | }
 285 | 
 286 | // Macro to verify/save a variable and log its position
 287 | #define DO_MAP(p, var) \
 288 |     do { \
 289 |         LogMap(p, #var); \
 290 |         p.Do(var); \
 291 |     } while(0)
 292 | 
 293 | // Macro for array elements (Fixing the snprintf cast error you saw earlier)
 294 | #define DO_MAP_ARRAY(p, arr, index) \
 295 |     do { \
 296 |         char name_buf[64]; \
 297 |         snprintf(name_buf, sizeof(name_buf), #arr "[%d]", static_cast<int>(index)); \
 298 |         LogMap(p, name_buf); \
 299 |         p.Do(arr[index]); \
 300 |     } while(0)
 301 | 
 302 | // DoWiiMixState is a custom savestate function that removes host data from savestates
 303 | // Making savestates more portable between different WiiMix configurations
 304 | static void DoWiiMixState(Core::System& system, PointerWrap& p)
 305 | {
 306 |   bool is_wii = system.IsWii() || system.IsMIOS();
 307 |   const bool is_wii_currently = is_wii;
 308 |   p.Do(is_wii);
 309 |   if (is_wii != is_wii_currently)
 310 |   {
 311 |     OSD::AddMessage(fmt::format("Cannot load a savestate created under {} mode in {} mode",
 312 |                                 is_wii ? "Wii" : "GC", is_wii_currently ? "Wii" : "GC"),
 313 |                     OSD::Duration::NORMAL, OSD::Color::RED);
 314 |     p.SetMeasureMode();
 315 |     return;
 316 |   }
 317 | 
 318 |   // Check to make sure the emulated memory sizes are the same as the savestate
 319 |   auto& memory = system.GetMemory();
 320 |   u32 state_mem1_size = memory.GetRamSizeReal();
 321 |   u32 state_mem2_size = memory.GetExRamSizeReal();
 322 |   p.Do(state_mem1_size);
 323 |   p.Do(state_mem2_size);
 324 |   if (state_mem1_size != memory.GetRamSizeReal() || state_mem2_size != memory.GetExRamSizeReal())
 325 |   {
 326 |     OSD::AddMessage(fmt::format("Memory size mismatch!\n"
 327 |                                 "Current | MEM1 {:08X} ({:3}MB)    MEM2 {:08X} ({:3}MB)\n"
 328 |                                 "State   | MEM1 {:08X} ({:3}MB)    MEM2 {:08X} ({:3}MB)",
 329 |                                 memory.GetRamSizeReal(), memory.GetRamSizeReal() / 0x100000U,
 330 |                                 memory.GetExRamSizeReal(), memory.GetExRamSizeReal() / 0x100000U,
 331 |                                 state_mem1_size, state_mem1_size / 0x100000U, state_mem2_size,
 332 |                                 state_mem2_size / 0x100000U));
 333 |     p.SetMeasureMode();
 334 |     return;
 335 |   }
 336 | 
 337 |   // Movie must be done before the video backend, because the window is redrawn in the video backend
 338 |   // state load, and the frame number must be up-to-date.
 339 | 
 340 |   // 100% pure emulated data; can be saved, may be useful in other places other than just Movie
 341 |   system.GetMovie().DoState(p);
 342 |   p.DoMarker("Movie");
 343 |   LogOffset("Movie", p);
 344 | 
 345 |   // Begin with video backend, so that it gets a chance to clear its caches and writeback modified
 346 |   // things to RAM
 347 | 
 348 |   // video backend is a MIX of emulated and host-specific data, so we need to modify it to only save emulated data
 349 |   // NOTE: video backend is difficult to separate, so for now I'm commenting it
 350 |   // g_video_backend->DoState(p);
 351 |   // p.DoMarker("video_backend");
 352 |   // LogOffset("video_backend", p);
 353 | 
 354 |   // 1. Backend Processor Registers
 355 |   // Contains ZMode, BlendMode, TevStages, etc.
 356 |   p.Do(bpmem);
 357 |   p.DoMarker("bpMem");
 358 |   LogOffset("bpMem", p);
 359 | 
 360 |   // 2. Transform Unit Memory
 361 |   // Contains Light Objects, Matrices, Viewport, etc.
 362 |   p.Do(xfmem);
 363 |   p.DoMarker("xfMem");
 364 |   LogOffset("xfMem", p);
 365 | 
 366 |   // 3. Command Processor State
 367 |   // Contains Vertex Attributes (VAT), current pipeline state
 368 |   if (p.IsMeasureMode()) printf("--- CP STATE BREAKDOWN ---\n");
 369 | 
 370 |   DO_MAP(p, g_main_cp_state.matrix_index_a.Hex);
 371 |   DO_MAP(p, g_main_cp_state.matrix_index_b.Hex);
 372 |   DO_MAP(p, g_main_cp_state.vtx_desc.low.Hex);
 373 |   DO_MAP(p, g_main_cp_state.vtx_desc.high.Hex);
 374 | 
 375 |   for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
 376 |       // Log the vtx_attr[i].g0.Hex field with its index
 377 |       {
 378 |         char name_buf[64];
 379 |         snprintf(name_buf, sizeof(name_buf), "vtx_attr[%d].g0.Hex", i);
 380 |         LogMap(p, name_buf);
 381 |         p.Do(g_main_cp_state.vtx_attr[i].g0.Hex);
 382 |       }
 383 |       // Actually, cleaner to just do manual logging for nested structs:
 384 |       // LogMap(p, fmt::format("vtx_attr[{}].g0", i).c_str()); p.Do(...);
 385 |       // But for now, let's focus on the arrays causing the crash.
 386 |   }
 387 | 
 388 |   // The Arrays (The culprit)
 389 |   for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) {
 390 |       CPArray idx = static_cast<CPArray>(i);
 391 |       DO_MAP_ARRAY(p, g_main_cp_state.array_bases, idx);
 392 |   }
 393 | 
 394 |   for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) {
 395 |       CPArray idx = static_cast<CPArray>(i);
 396 |       DO_MAP_ARRAY(p, g_main_cp_state.array_strides, idx);
 397 |   }
 398 | 
 399 |   p.Do(g_preprocess_cp_state);
 400 |   p.DoMarker("cpMem");
 401 |   LogOffset("cpMem", p);
 402 | 
 403 |   // CoreTiming needs to be restored before restoring Hardware because
 404 |   // the controller code might need to schedule an event if the controller has changed.
 405 |   // CoreTiming has 2 distinct parts:
 406 |   // - Emulated data (timing, scheduled events)
 407 |   // - Host data (m_event_types (maps events to function pointers; host-specific))
 408 |   system.GetCoreTiming().LogTimingState("Before DoWiiMixState CoreTiming");
 409 |   system.GetCoreTiming().DoState(p);
 410 |   p.DoMarker("CoreTiming");
 411 |   LogOffset("CoreTiming", p);
 412 |   system.GetCoreTiming().LogTimingState("After DoWiiMixState CoreTiming");
 413 | 
 414 |   // HW needs to be restored before PowerPC because the data cache might need to be flushed.
 415 |   // TODOx: HW::DoState may include host-specific data, so we may need to modify it for WiiMix
 416 | 
 417 |   // NOTE: HW currently only saves mem
 418 |   HW::DoState(system, p);
 419 |   p.DoMarker("HW");
 420 |   LogOffset("HW", p);
 421 | 
 422 |   // TODOx: PowerPC::DoState serializes JIT, so we should include a flag to skip that for WiiMix savestates
 423 |   // TODOx: bring this back once I can figure it out
 424 |   system.GetPowerPC().DoState(p);
 425 |   p.DoMarker("PowerPC");
 426 |   LogOffset("PowerPC", p);
 427 | 
 428 |   // TODOx: Likely has bluetooth host-specific data
 429 |   if (system.IsWii()) {
 430 |     Wiimote::DoState(p);
 431 |     p.DoMarker("Wiimote");
 432 |     LogOffset("Wiimote", p);
 433 |   }
 434 |   // Gecko codes are currently not supported for WiiMix
 435 |   if (!WIIMIX_STATE) {
 436 |     Gecko::DoState(p);
 437 |     p.DoMarker("Gecko");
 438 |     LogOffset("Gecko", p);
 439 |   }
 440 | 
 441 |   // Retroachievements DoState is actually required
 442 |   // It only saves emulated data (achievement progress)
 443 |   // if (!WIIMIX_STATE) {
 444 |   #ifdef USE_RETRO_ACHIEVEMENTS
 445 |     AchievementManager::GetInstance().DoState(p);
 446 |     p.DoMarker("AchievementManager");
 447 |     LogOffset("AchievementManager", p);
 448 |   #endif  // USE_RETRO_ACHIEVEMENTS
 449 |   // }
 450 | }
 451 | 
 452 | void LoadFromBuffer(Core::System& system, std::vector<u8>& buffer)
 453 | {
 454 |   if (NetPlay::IsNetPlayRunning())
 455 |   {
 456 |     OSD::AddMessage("Loading savestates is disabled in Netplay to prevent desyncs");
 457 |     return;
 458 |   }
 459 | 
 460 |   if (AchievementManager::GetInstance().IsHardcoreModeActive())
 461 |   {
 462 |     OSD::AddMessage("Loading savestates is disabled in RetroAchievements hardcore mode");
 463 |     return;
 464 |   }
 465 | 
 466 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(
 467 |       system,
 468 |       [&] {
 469 |         u8* ptr = buffer.data();
 470 |         PointerWrap p(&ptr, buffer.size(), PointerWrap::Mode::Read);
 471 |         DoState(system, p);
 472 |       },
 473 |       true);
 474 | }
 475 | 
 476 | bool WiiMixLoadFromBuffer(Core::System& system, std::vector<u8>& buffer)
 477 | {
 478 |   if (NetPlay::IsNetPlayRunning())
 479 |   {
 480 |     OSD::AddMessage("Loading savestates is disabled in Netplay to prevent desyncs");
 481 |     return false;
 482 |   }
 483 | 
 484 |   if (AchievementManager::GetInstance().IsHardcoreModeActive())
 485 |   {
 486 |     OSD::AddMessage("Loading savestates is disabled in RetroAchievements hardcore mode");
 487 |     return false;
 488 |   }
 489 | 
 490 |   // This should only be called from the CPU thread
 491 |   bool success = false;
 492 |   u8* ptr = buffer.data();
 493 |   PointerWrap p(&ptr, buffer.size(), PointerWrap::Mode::Read);
 494 |   DoWiiMixState(system, p);
 495 | 
 496 |   if (p.IsReadMode())
 497 |   {
 498 |     success = WiiMixHostReinitialization(system);
 499 |   }
 500 |   return success;
 501 | }
 502 | 
 503 | bool WiiMixLoadFromBufferEmuThread(Core::System& system, std::vector<u8>& buffer)
 504 | {
 505 |     if (NetPlay::IsNetPlayRunning())
 506 |     {
 507 |         OSD::AddMessage("Loading savestates is disabled in Netplay to prevent desyncs");
 508 |         return false;
 509 |     }
 510 | 
 511 |     if (AchievementManager::GetInstance().IsHardcoreModeActive())
 512 |     {
 513 |         OSD::AddMessage("Loading savestates is disabled in RetroAchievements hardcore mode");
 514 |         return false;
 515 |     }
 516 | 
 517 |     // This should only be called from the CPU thread
 518 | 
 519 |     bool success = false;
 520 |     u8* ptr = buffer.data();
 521 |     PointerWrap p(&ptr, buffer.size(), PointerWrap::Mode::Read);
 522 | 
 523 |     auto& power_pc = system.GetPowerPC();
 524 | 
 525 |     system.GetCoreTiming().LogEventQueues("Start of Load Function");
 526 | 
 527 |     // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 528 |     // STEP 1: HOST RE-INITIALIZATION (RESET)
 529 |     // We must reset the emulated hardware and host state BEFORE loading data.
 530 |     // If we do this after, we wipe out the data we just loaded (causing the cpMem mismatches).
 531 |     // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 532 |     if (p.IsReadMode()) {
 533 |         // 1. Poison memory/state to ensure we aren't relying on stale data
 534 |         system.PoisonState(static_cast<Core::PoisonModule>(State::s_poison_mask));
 535 | 
 536 |         // 2. Reset Host Environment (Hardware, Timers, Threads)
 537 |         // This registers all Event Types but leaves queues empty/default.
 538 |         success = WiiMixHostReinitialization(system);
 539 |         if (!success)
 540 |             return false;
 541 | 
 542 |         system.GetCoreTiming().LogEventQueues("After Host Reinit");
 543 | 
 544 |         // 3. Clear the Event Queue
 545 |         // WiiMixReset might have added default startup events. We don't want those;
 546 |         // we want exactly what is in the save file.
 547 |         system.GetCoreTiming().MoveEvents();
 548 |         system.GetCoreTiming().LogEventQueues("After MoveEvents");
 549 |         system.GetCoreTiming().ClearPendingEvents();
 550 |         system.GetCoreTiming().LogEventQueues("After ClearPendingEvents");
 551 | 
 552 |         // 4. Clear Critical Hardware State Manually
 553 |         // Even though HostReinit does this, we double-tap CP state to be safe.
 554 |         std::memset(&g_main_cp_state, 0, sizeof(g_main_cp_state));
 555 |         std::memset(&g_preprocess_cp_state, 0, sizeof(g_preprocess_cp_state));
 556 |     }
 557 | 
 558 |     printf("Current frame in EmuThread before DoWiiMixState: %lu\n", system.GetMovie().GetCurrentFrame());
 559 | 
 560 |     // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 561 |     // STEP 2: LOAD STATE
 562 |     // Now that the machine is "fresh" and all Event Types are registered,
 563 |     // we assume the authoritative state from the buffer.
 564 |     // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 565 |     DoWiiMixState(system, p);
 566 | 
 567 |     // --- DIAGNOSTIC TRAP: IMMEDIATE READBACK ---
 568 |     if (g_main_cp_state.matrix_index_a.Hex == 0xFFFFFFFF) {
 569 |         printf("CRITICAL FAILURE: CP State corrupted IMMEDIATELY after DoWiiMixState (Load)!\n");
 570 |     } else {
 571 |         printf("CP State clean after DoWiiMixState: 0x%08X\n", g_main_cp_state.matrix_index_a.Hex);
 572 |     }
 573 |     // ---------------------------------------------
 574 | 
 575 |     system.GetCoreTiming().LogEventQueues("After DoWiiMixState (Pure State Loaded)");
 576 | 
 577 |     // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 578 |     // STEP 3: POST-LOAD THREAD RESTART
 579 |     // Restart any threads that were stopped during Reset.
 580 |     // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 581 |     if (p.IsReadMode()) {
 582 |         // DVD Interface (Restarts the DVD Thread)
 583 |         system.GetDVDInterface().WiiMixRestart();
 584 | 
 585 |         // DSP (Restarts the DSP Thread)
 586 |         system.GetDSP().WiiMixRestart(Config::Get<bool>(Config::MAIN_DSP_HLE));
 587 |     }
 588 | 
 589 |     // Refresh cpu base pointer to point to valid JIT
 590 |     power_pc.ApplyMode();
 591 | 
 592 |     auto& ppc_state = power_pc.GetPPCState();
 593 |     auto& memory = system.GetMemory();
 594 | 
 595 |     // CRITICAL: Force the JIT/Code Cache to clear.
 596 |     {
 597 |         Core::CPUThreadGuard guard(system);
 598 |         system.GetJitInterface().ClearCache(guard);
 599 |     }
 600 | 
 601 |     if (!ppc_state.m_enable_dcache)
 602 |     {
 603 |         INFO_LOG_FMT(POWERPC, "Flushing data cache");
 604 |         ppc_state.dCache.FlushAll(memory);
 605 |     }
 606 | 
 607 |     PowerPC::RoundingModeUpdated(ppc_state);
 608 |     PowerPC::RecalculateAllFeatureFlags(ppc_state);
 609 | 
 610 |     auto& mmu = system.GetMMU();
 611 |     mmu.IBATUpdated();
 612 |     mmu.DBATUpdated();
 613 | 
 614 |     // Prevent CoreTiming from running a full advance right after loading the state
 615 |     auto& core_timing = system.GetCoreTiming();
 616 |     if (ppc_state.downcount > 0 && ppc_state.downcount < 20000) {
 617 |         core_timing.SetSkipNextAdvance(true);
 618 |     } else {
 619 |         core_timing.SetSkipNextAdvance(false);
 620 |     }
 621 | 
 622 |     if (p.IsReadMode()) {
 623 |             // A. Invalidate Vertex Loader
 624 |             VertexLoaderManager::Init();
 625 | 
 626 |             // FIX: Ensure XF Memory is invalidated BEFORE Shader/Vertex Managers
 627 |             auto& xf = system.GetXFStateManager();
 628 |             xf.InvalidateXFRange(0, 0x1000); // 4KB XF memory forces backend to reload all XF data
 629 |             xf.SetProjectionChanged();
 630 |             xf.SetViewportChanged();
 631 |             xf.SetLightingConfigChanged();
 632 | 
 633 |             if (g_vertex_manager) {
 634 |                 g_vertex_manager->Invalidate();
 635 |             }
 636 | 
 637 |             // B. Dirty the Shader Managers
 638 |             system.GetVertexShaderManager().dirty = true;
 639 |             system.GetPixelShaderManager().Dirty();
 640 |             system.GetGeometryShaderManager().Dirty();
 641 |     }
 642 | 
 643 |     printf("Current frame at end of EmuThread: %lu\n", system.GetMovie().GetCurrentFrame());
 644 |     system.GetCoreTiming().LogEventQueues("After Cache Refresh");
 645 | 
 646 |     return p.IsReadMode();
 647 | }
 648 | 
 649 | void SaveToBuffer(Core::System& system, std::vector<u8>& buffer)
 650 | {
 651 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(
 652 |       system,
 653 |       [&] {
 654 |         u8* ptr = nullptr;
 655 |         PointerWrap p_measure(&ptr, 0, PointerWrap::Mode::Measure);
 656 | 
 657 |         DoState(system, p_measure);
 658 |         const size_t buffer_size = reinterpret_cast<size_t>(ptr);
 659 |         buffer.resize(buffer_size);
 660 | 
 661 |         ptr = buffer.data();
 662 |         PointerWrap p(&ptr, buffer_size, PointerWrap::Mode::Write);
 663 |         DoState(system, p);
 664 |       },
 665 |       true);
 666 | }
 667 | 
 668 | void WiiMixSaveToBuffer(Core::System& system, std::vector<u8>& buffer)
 669 | {
 670 |     // Should be called from CPU thread already
 671 |     u8* ptr = nullptr;
 672 |     PointerWrap p_measure(&ptr, 0, PointerWrap::Mode::Measure);
 673 | 
 674 |     // Measure
 675 |     DoWiiMixState(system, p_measure);
 676 |     const size_t buffer_size = reinterpret_cast<size_t>(ptr);
 677 |     buffer.resize(buffer_size);
 678 | 
 679 |     ptr = buffer.data();
 680 |     PointerWrap p(&ptr, buffer_size, PointerWrap::Mode::Write);
 681 | 
 682 |     // Write
 683 |     DoWiiMixState(system, p);
 684 | }

 692 | };
 693 | }  // namespace
 694 | 
 695 | // returns first slot number not in the vector, or -1 if all are in the vector
 696 | static int GetEmptySlot(const std::vector<SlotWithTimestamp>& used_slots)
 697 | {
 698 |   for (int i = 1; i <= (int)NUM_STATES; i++)
 699 |   {
 700 |     const auto it = std::find_if(used_slots.begin(), used_slots.end(),
 701 |                                  [i](const SlotWithTimestamp& slot) { return slot.slot == i; });
 702 |     if (it == used_slots.end())
 703 |       return i;
 704 |   }
 705 |   return -1;
 706 | }
 707 | 
 708 | // Arbitrarily chosen value (38 years) that is subtracted in GetSystemTimeAsDouble()
 709 | // to increase sub-second precision of the resulting double timestamp
 710 | static constexpr int DOUBLE_TIME_OFFSET = (38 * 365 * 24 * 60 * 60);
 711 | 
 712 | static double GetSystemTimeAsDouble()
 713 | {
 714 |   const auto since_epoch = std::chrono::system_clock::now().time_since_epoch();
 715 | 
 716 |   const auto since_double_time_epoch = since_epoch - std::chrono::seconds(DOUBLE_TIME_OFFSET);
 717 |   return std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::duration<double>>(since_double_time_epoch).count();
 718 | }
 719 | 
 720 | static std::string SystemTimeAsDoubleToString(double time)
 721 | {
 722 |   // revert adjustments from GetSystemTimeAsDouble() to get a normal Unix timestamp again
 723 |   const time_t seconds = static_cast<time_t>(time) + DOUBLE_TIME_OFFSET;
 724 |   const auto local_time = Common::Localtime(seconds);
 725 |   if (!local_time)
 726 |     return "";
 727 | 
 728 |   // fmt is locale agnostic by default, so explicitly use current locale.
 729 |   return fmt::format(std::locale{""}, "{:%x %X}", *local_time);
 730 | }
 731 | 
 732 | static std::string MakeStateFilename(int number);
 733 | 
 734 | static std::vector<SlotWithTimestamp> GetUsedSlotsWithTimestamp()
 735 | {
 736 |   std::vector<SlotWithTimestamp> result;
 737 |   StateHeader header;
 738 |   for (int i = 1; i <= (int)NUM_STATES; i++)
 739 |   {
 740 |     std::string filename = MakeStateFilename(i);
 741 |     if (File::Exists(filename))
 742 |     {
 743 |       if (ReadHeader(filename, header))
 744 |       {
 745 |         result.emplace_back(SlotWithTimestamp{.slot = i, .timestamp = header.legacy_header.time});
 746 |       }
 747 |     }
 748 |   }
 749 |   return result;
 750 | }
 751 | 
 752 | static bool CompareTimestamp(const SlotWithTimestamp& lhs, const SlotWithTimestamp& rhs)
 753 | {
 754 |   return lhs.timestamp < rhs.timestamp;
 755 | }
 756 | 
 757 | static void CompressBufferToFile(const u8* raw_buffer, u64 size, File::IOFile& f)
 758 | {
 759 |   u64 total_bytes_compressed = 0;
 760 | 
 761 |   while (true)
 762 |   {
 763 |     u64 bytes_left_to_compress = size - total_bytes_compressed;
 764 | 
 765 |     int bytes_to_compress =
 766 |         static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<u64>(LZ4_MAX_INPUT_SIZE), bytes_left_to_compress));
 767 |     int compressed_buffer_size = LZ4_compressBound(bytes_to_compress);
 768 |     auto compressed_buffer = std::make_unique<char[]>(compressed_buffer_size);
 769 |     s32 compressed_len =
 770 |         LZ4_compress_default(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(raw_buffer) + total_bytes_compressed,
 771 |                              compressed_buffer.get(), bytes_to_compress, compressed_buffer_size);
 772 | 
 773 |     if (compressed_len == 0)
 774 |     {
 775 |       PanicAlertFmtT("Internal LZ4 Error - compression failed");
 776 |       break;
 777 |     }
 778 | 
 779 |     // The size of the data to write is 'compressed_len'
 780 |     f.WriteArray(&compressed_len, 1);
 781 |     f.WriteBytes(compressed_buffer.get(), compressed_len);
 782 | 
 783 |     total_bytes_compressed += bytes_to_compress;
 784 |     if (total_bytes_compressed == size)
 785 |       break;
 786 |   }
 787 | }
 788 | 
 789 | static void CreateExtendedHeader(StateExtendedHeader& extended_header, size_t uncompressed_size)
 790 | {
 791 |   StateExtendedBaseHeader& base_header = extended_header.base_header;
 792 |   base_header.header_version = EXTENDED_HEADER_VERSION;
 793 |   base_header.compression_type =
 794 |       s_use_compression ? CompressionType::LZ4 : CompressionType::Uncompressed;
 795 |   base_header.payload_offset = COMPRESSED_DATA_OFFSET;
 796 |   base_header.uncompressed_size = uncompressed_size;
 797 | 
 798 |   // If more fields are added to StateExtendedHeader, set them here.
 799 | }
 800 | 
 801 | static void WriteHeadersToFile(size_t uncompressed_size, File::IOFile& f)
 802 | {
 803 |   StateHeader header{};
 804 |   SConfig::GetInstance().GetGameID().copy(header.legacy_header.game_id,
 805 |                                           std::size(header.legacy_header.game_id));
 806 |   header.legacy_header.time = GetSystemTimeAsDouble();
 807 | 
 808 |   header.version_header.version_cookie = WIIMIX_COOKIE_BASE + WIIMIX_STATE_VERSION;
 809 |   header.version_string = Common::GetScmRevStr();
 810 |   header.version_header.version_string_length = static_cast<u32>(header.version_string.length());
 811 | 
 812 |   StateExtendedHeader extended_header{};
 813 |   CreateExtendedHeader(extended_header, uncompressed_size);
 814 | 
 815 |   f.WriteArray(&header.legacy_header, 1);
 816 |   f.WriteArray(&header.version_header, 1);
 817 |   f.WriteString(header.version_string);
 818 | 
 819 |   f.WriteArray(&extended_header.base_header, 1);
 820 |   // If StateExtendedHeader is amended to include more than the base, add WriteBytes() calls here.
 821 | }
 822 | 
 823 | static void WriteWiiMixHeadersToFile(size_t uncompressed_size, File::IOFile& f)
 824 | {
 825 |   StateHeader header{};
 826 |   SConfig::GetInstance().GetGameID().copy(header.legacy_header.game_id,
 827 |                                           std::size(header.legacy_header.game_id));
 828 |   header.legacy_header.time = GetSystemTimeAsDouble();
 829 | 
 830 |   header.version_header.version_cookie = WIIMIX_COOKIE_BASE + WIIMIX_STATE_VERSION;
 831 |   header.version_string = Common::GetScmRevStr();
 832 |   header.version_header.version_string_length = static_cast<u32>(header.version_string.length());
 833 | 
 834 |   StateExtendedHeader extended_header{};
 835 |   CreateExtendedHeader(extended_header, uncompressed_size);
 836 | 
 837 |   f.WriteArray(&header.legacy_header, 1);
 838 |   f.WriteArray(&header.version_header, 1);
 839 |   f.WriteString(header.version_string);
 840 | 
 841 |   f.WriteArray(&extended_header.base_header, 1);
 842 |   // If StateExtendedHeader is amended to include more than the base, add WriteBytes() calls here.
 843 | }
 844 | 
 845 | static void CompressAndDumpState(Core::System& system, CompressAndDumpState_args& save_args)
 846 | {
 847 |   const u8* const buffer_data = save_args.buffer_vector.data();
 848 |   const size_t buffer_size = save_args.buffer_vector.size();
 849 |   const std::string& filename = save_args.filename;
 850 | 
 851 |   // Find free temporary filename.
 852 |   // TODO: The file exists check and the actual opening of the file should be atomic, we don't have
 853 |   // functions for that.
 854 |   std::string temp_filename;
 855 |   size_t temp_counter = static_cast<size_t>(Common::CurrentThreadId());
 856 |   do
 857 |   {
 858 |     temp_filename = fmt::format("{}{}.tmp", filename, temp_counter);
 859 |     ++temp_counter;
 860 |   } while (File::Exists(temp_filename));
 861 | 
 862 |   File::IOFile f(temp_filename, "wb");
 863 |   if (!f)
 864 |   {
 865 |     Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to create state file", 2000);
 866 |     return;
 867 |   }
 868 | 
 869 |   WriteHeadersToFile(buffer_size, f);
 870 | 
 871 |   if (s_use_compression)
 872 |     CompressBufferToFile(buffer_data, buffer_size, f);
 873 |   else
 874 |     f.WriteBytes(buffer_data, buffer_size);
 875 | 
 876 |   if (!f.IsGood())
 877 |     Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to write state file", 2000);
 878 | 
 879 |   const std::string last_state_filename = File::GetUserPath(D_STATESAVES_IDX) + "lastState.sav";
 880 |   const std::string last_state_dtmname = last_state_filename + ".dtm";
 881 |   const std::string dtmname = filename + ".dtm";
 882 | 
 883 |   {
 884 |     std::lock_guard lk(s_save_thread_mutex);
 885 | 
 886 |     // Backup existing state (overwriting an existing backup, if any).
 887 |     if (File::Exists(filename))
 888 |     {
 889 |       if (File::Exists(last_state_filename))
 890 |         File::Delete((last_state_filename));
 891 |       if (File::Exists(last_state_dtmname))
 892 |         File::Delete((last_state_dtmname));
 893 | 
 894 |       if (!File::Rename(filename, last_state_filename))
 895 |       {
 896 |         Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to move previous state to state undo backup", 1000);
 897 |       }
 898 |       else if (File::Exists(dtmname))
 899 |       {
 900 |         if (!File::Rename(dtmname, last_state_dtmname))
 901 |           Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to move previous state's dtm to state undo backup", 1000);
 902 |       }
 903 |     }
 904 | 
 905 |     auto& movie = system.GetMovie();
 906 |     if ((movie.IsMovieActive()) && !movie.IsJustStartingRecordingInputFromSaveState())
 907 |       movie.SaveRecording(dtmname);
 908 |     else if (!movie.IsMovieActive())
 909 |       File::Delete(dtmname);
 910 | 
 911 |     // Move written state to final location.
 912 |     // TODO: This should also be atomic. This is possible on all systems, but needs a special
 913 |     // implementation of IOFile on Windows.
 914 |     if (!f.Close())
 915 |       Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to close state file", 2000);
 916 | 
 917 |     if (!File::Rename(temp_filename, filename))
 918 |     {
 919 |       Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to rename state file", 2000);
 920 |     }
 921 |     else
 922 |     {
 923 |       const std::filesystem::path temp_path(filename);
 924 |       Core::DisplayMessage(fmt::format("Saved State to {}", temp_path.filename().string()), 2000);
 925 |     }
 926 |   }
 927 | 
 928 |   Host_UpdateMainFrame();
 929 |   safe_to_quit = true;
 930 | }
 931 | 
 932 | static void CompressAndDumpWiiMixState(Core::System& system, CompressAndDumpState_args& save_args)
 933 | {
 934 |   const u8* const buffer_data = save_args.buffer_vector.data();
 935 |   const size_t buffer_size = save_args.buffer_vector.size();
 936 |   const std::string& filename = save_args.filename;
 937 | 
 938 |   // Find free temporary filename.
 939 |   // TODO: The file exists check and the actual opening of the file should be atomic, we don't have
 940 |   // functions for that.
 941 |   std::string temp_filename;
 942 |   size_t temp_counter = static_cast<size_t>(Common::CurrentThreadId());
 943 |   do
 944 |   {
 945 |     temp_filename = fmt::format("{}{}.tmp", filename, temp_counter);
 946 |     ++temp_counter;
 947 |   } while (File::Exists(temp_filename));
 948 | 
 949 |   File::IOFile f(temp_filename, "wb");
 950 |   if (!f)
 951 |   {
 952 |     Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to create state file", 2000);
 953 |     return;
 954 |   }
 955 | 
 956 |   WriteWiiMixHeadersToFile(buffer_size, f);
 957 | 
 958 |   if (s_use_compression)
 959 |     CompressBufferToFile(buffer_data, buffer_size, f);
 960 |   else
 961 |     f.WriteBytes(buffer_data, buffer_size);
 962 | 
 963 |   if (!f.IsGood())
 964 |     Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to write state file", 2000);
 965 | 
 966 |   const std::string last_state_filename = File::GetUserPath(D_STATESAVES_IDX) + "lastState." + WIIMIX_STATE_EXTENSION;
 967 |   const std::string last_state_dtmname = last_state_filename + ".dtm";
 968 |   const std::string dtmname = filename + ".dtm";
 969 | 
 970 |   {
 971 |     std::lock_guard lk(s_save_thread_mutex);
 972 | 
 973 |     // Backup existing state (overwriting an existing backup, if any).
 974 |     if (File::Exists(filename))
 975 |     {
 976 |       if (File::Exists(last_state_filename))
 977 |         File::Delete((last_state_filename));
 978 |       if (File::Exists(last_state_dtmname))
 979 |         File::Delete((last_state_dtmname));
 980 | 
 981 |       if (!File::Rename(filename, last_state_filename))
 982 |       {
 983 |         Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to move previous state to state undo backup", 1000);
 984 |       }
 985 |       else if (File::Exists(dtmname))
 986 |       {
 987 |         if (!File::Rename(dtmname, last_state_dtmname))
 988 |           Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to move previous state's dtm to state undo backup", 1000);
 989 |       }
 990 |     }
 991 | 
 992 |     auto& movie = system.GetMovie();
 993 |     if ((movie.IsMovieActive()) && !movie.IsJustStartingRecordingInputFromSaveState())
 994 |       movie.SaveRecording(dtmname);
 995 |     else if (!movie.IsMovieActive())
 996 |       File::Delete(dtmname);
 997 | 
 998 |     // Move written state to final location.
 999 |     // TODO: This should also be atomic. This is possible on all systems, but needs a special
1000 |     // implementation of IOFile on Windows.
1001 |     if (!f.Close())
1002 |       Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to close state file", 2000);
1003 | 
1004 |     if (!File::Rename(temp_filename, filename))
1005 |     {
1006 |       Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to rename state file", 2000);
1007 |     }
1008 |     else
1009 |     {
1010 |       const std::filesystem::path temp_path(filename);
1011 |       Core::DisplayMessage(fmt::format("Saved State to {}", temp_path.filename().string()), 2000);
1012 |     }
1013 |   }
1014 | 
1015 |   Host_UpdateMainFrame();
1016 |   safe_to_quit = true;
1017 | }
1018 | 
1019 | void SaveAs(Core::System& system, const std::string& filename, bool wait)
1020 | {
1021 |   std::unique_lock lk(s_load_or_save_in_progress_mutex, std::try_to_lock);
1022 |   if (!lk)
1023 |     return;
1024 |   safe_to_quit = false;
1025 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(
1026 |       system,
1027 |       [&] {
1028 |         Core::safe_to_quit = false;
1029 |         {
1030 |           std::lock_guard lk_(s_state_writes_in_queue_mutex);
1031 |           ++s_state_writes_in_queue;
1032 |         }
1033 | 
1034 |         // Measure the size of the buffer.
1035 |         u8* ptr = nullptr;
1036 |         PointerWrap p_measure(&ptr, 0, PointerWrap::Mode::Measure);
1037 |         DoState(system, p_measure);
1038 |         const size_t buffer_size = reinterpret_cast<size_t>(ptr);
1039 | 
1040 |         // Then actually do the write.
1041 |         std::vector<u8> current_buffer;
1042 |         current_buffer.resize(buffer_size);
1043 |         ptr = current_buffer.data();
1044 |         PointerWrap p(&ptr, buffer_size, PointerWrap::Mode::Write);
1045 |         DoState(system, p);
1046 | 
1047 |         if (p.IsWriteMode())
1048 |         {
1049 |           Core::DisplayMessage("Saving State...", 1000);
1050 | 
1051 |           std::shared_ptr<Common::Event> sync_event;
1052 | 
1053 |           CompressAndDumpState_args save_args;
1054 |           save_args.buffer_vector = std::move(current_buffer);
1055 |           save_args.filename = filename;
1056 |           if (wait)
1057 |           {
1058 |             sync_event = std::make_shared<Common::Event>();
1059 |             save_args.state_write_done_event = sync_event;
1060 |           }
1061 | 
1062 |           s_save_thread.EmplaceItem(std::move(save_args));
1063 | 
1064 |           if (sync_event)
1065 |             sync_event->Wait();
1066 |         }
1067 |         else
1068 |         {
1069 |           // someone aborted the save by changing the mode?
1070 |           {
1071 |             // Note: The worker thread takes care of this in the other branch.
1072 |             std::lock_guard lk_(s_state_writes_in_queue_mutex);
1073 |             if (--s_state_writes_in_queue == 0)
1074 |               s_state_write_queue_is_empty.notify_all();
1075 |           }
1076 |           Core::DisplayMessage("Unable to save: Internal DoState Error", 4000);
1077 |         }
1078 |         Core::safe_to_quit = true;
1079 |       },
1080 |       true);
1081 |   // safe_to_quit = true;
1082 | }
1083 | 
1084 | void SaveAsWiiMix(Core::System& system, const std::string& filename, bool wait)
1085 | {
1086 |   std::unique_lock lk(s_load_or_save_in_progress_mutex, std::try_to_lock);
1087 |   if (!lk)
1088 |     return;

1092 |       [&] {

1096 |           ++s_state_writes_in_queue;
1097 |         }
1098 |

1100 |         u8* ptr = nullptr;

1102 |         DoWiiMixState(system, p_measure);
1103 |         const size_t buffer_size = reinterpret_cast<size_t>(ptr);
1104 |

1110 |         DoWiiMixState(system, p);
1111 | 
1112 |         if (p.IsWriteMode())
1113 |         {
1114 |           Core::DisplayMessage("Saving State...", 1000);
1115 | 
1116 |           std::shared_ptr<Common::Event> sync_event;
1117 | 
1118 |           CompressAndDumpState_args save_args;
1119 |           save_args.buffer_vector = std::move(current_buffer);
1120 |           save_args.filename = filename;
1121 |           if (wait)
1122 |           {
1123 |             sync_event = std::make_shared<Common::Event>();
1124 |             save_args.state_write_done_event = sync_event;
1125 |           }
1126 | 
1127 |           s_wiimix_save_thread.EmplaceItem(std::move(save_args));
1128 | 
1129 |           if (sync_event)
1130 |             sync_event->Wait();
1131 |         }
1132 |         else
1133 |         {
1134 |           // someone aborted the save by changing the mode?
1135 |           {
1136 |             // Note: The worker thread takes care of this in the other branch.
1137 |             std::lock_guard lk_(s_state_writes_in_queue_mutex);
1138 |             if (--s_state_writes_in_queue == 0)
1139 |               s_state_write_queue_is_empty.notify_all();
1140 |           }
1141 |           Core::DisplayMessage("Unable to save: Internal DoState Error", 4000);
1142 |         }
1143 |         Core::safe_to_quit = true;
1144 |       },
1145 |       true);
1146 |   // safe_to_quit = true;
1147 | }
1148 | 
1149 | static bool GetVersionFromLZO(StateHeader& header, File::IOFile& f)
1150 | {
1151 |   // Just read the first block, since it will contain the full revision string
1152 |   lzo_uint32 cur_len = 0;  // size of compressed bytes
1153 |   lzo_uint new_len = 0;    // size of uncompressed bytes
1154 |   std::vector<u8> buffer;
1155 |   buffer.resize(header.legacy_header.lzo_size);
1156 | 
1157 |   if (!f.ReadArray(&cur_len, 1) || !f.ReadBytes(out, cur_len))
1158 |     return false;
1159 | 
1160 |   const int res = lzo1x_decompress(out, cur_len, buffer.data(), &new_len, nullptr);
1161 |   if (res != LZO_E_OK)
1162 |   {
1163 |     // This doesn't seem to happen anymore.
1164 |     PanicAlertFmtT("Internal LZO Error - decompression failed ({0}) ({1}) \n"
1165 |                    "Unable to retrieve outdated savestate version info.",
1166 |                    res, new_len);
1167 |     return false;
1168 |   }
1169 | 
1170 |   // Read in cookie and string length
1171 |   if (buffer.size() >= sizeof(StateHeaderVersion))
1172 |   {
1173 |     memcpy(&header.version_header, buffer.data(), sizeof(StateHeaderVersion));
1174 |   }
1175 |   else
1176 |   {
1177 |     PanicAlertFmtT("Internal LZO Error - failed to parse decompressed version cookie and version "
1178 |                    "string length ({0})",
1179 |                    buffer.size());
1180 |     return false;
1181 |   }
1182 | 
1183 |   // Read in the string
1184 |   if (buffer.size() >= sizeof(StateHeaderVersion) + header.version_header.version_string_length)
1185 |   {
1186 |     auto version_buffer = std::make_unique<char[]>(header.version_header.version_string_length);
1187 |     memcpy(version_buffer.get(), buffer.data() + sizeof(StateHeaderVersion),
1188 |            header.version_header.version_string_length);
1189 |     header.version_string =
1190 |         std::string(version_buffer.get(), header.version_header.version_string_length);
1191 |   }
1192 |   else
1193 |   {
1194 |     PanicAlertFmtT("Internal LZO Error - failed to parse decompressed version string ({0} / {1})",
1195 |                    header.version_header.version_string_length, buffer.size());
1196 |     return false;
1197 |   }
1198 | 
1199 |   return true;
1200 | }
1201 | 
1202 | static bool ReadStateHeaderFromFile(StateHeader& header, File::IOFile& f,
1203 |                                     bool get_version_header = true)
1204 | {
1205 |   if (!f.IsOpen())
1206 |   {
1207 |     Core::DisplayMessage("State not found", 2000);
1208 |     return false;
1209 |   }
1210 | 
1211 |   if (!f.ReadArray(&header.legacy_header, 1))
1212 |   {
1213 |     Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to read state legacy header", 2000);
1214 |     return false;
1215 |   }
1216 | 
1217 |   // Bail out if we only care for retrieving the legacy header.
1218 |   // This is the case with ReadHeader() calls.
1219 |   if (!get_version_header)
1220 |     return true;
1221 | 
1222 |   if (header.legacy_header.lzo_size != 0)
1223 |   {
1224 |     // Parse out version from legacy LZO compressed states
1225 |     if (!GetVersionFromLZO(header, f))
1226 |       return false;
1227 |   }
1228 |   else
1229 |   {
1230 |     if (!f.ReadArray(&header.version_header, 1))
1231 |     {
1232 |       Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to read state version header", 2000);
1233 |       return false;
1234 |     }
1235 | 
1236 |     auto version_buffer = std::make_unique<char[]>(header.version_header.version_string_length);
1237 |     if (!f.ReadBytes(version_buffer.get(), header.version_header.version_string_length))
1238 |     {
1239 |       Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to read state version string", 2000);
1240 |       return false;
1241 |     }
1242 | 
1243 |     header.version_string =
1244 |         std::string(version_buffer.get(), header.version_header.version_string_length);
1245 |   }
1246 | 
1247 |   return true;
1248 | }
1249 | 
1250 | static bool ReadWiiMixStateHeaderFromFile(StateHeader& header, File::IOFile& f,
1251 |                                     bool get_version_header = true)
1252 | {
1253 |   if (!f.IsOpen())
1254 |   {
1255 |     Core::DisplayMessage("State not found", 2000);
1256 |     return false;
1257 |   }
1258 | 
1259 |   if (!f.ReadArray(&header.legacy_header, 1))
1260 |   {
1261 |     Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to read state legacy header", 2000);
1262 |     return false;
1263 |   }
1264 | 
1265 |   // Bail out if we only care for retrieving the legacy header.
1266 |   // This is the case with ReadHeader() calls.
1267 |   if (!get_version_header)
1268 |     return true;
1269 | 
1270 |   if (header.legacy_header.lzo_size != 0)
1271 |   {
1272 |     // Parse out version from legacy LZO compressed states
1273 |     if (!GetVersionFromLZO(header, f))
1274 |       return false;
1275 |   }
1276 |   else
1277 |   {
1278 |     if (!f.ReadArray(&header.version_header, 1))
1279 |     {
1280 |       Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to read state version header", 2000);
1281 |       return false;
1282 |     }
1283 | 
1284 |     auto version_buffer = std::make_unique<char[]>(header.version_header.version_string_length);
1285 |     if (!f.ReadBytes(version_buffer.get(), header.version_header.version_string_length))
1286 |     {
1287 |       Core::DisplayMessage("Failed to read state version string", 2000);
1288 |       return false;
1289 |     }
1290 | 
1291 |     header.version_string =
1292 |         std::string(version_buffer.get(), header.version_header.version_string_length);
1293 |   }
1294 | 
1295 |   return true;
1296 | }
1297 | 
1298 | bool ReadHeader(const std::string& filename, StateHeader& header)
1299 | {
1300 |   // ensure that the savestate write thread isn't moving around states while we do this
1301 |   std::lock_guard lk(s_save_thread_mutex);
1302 | 
1303 |   File::IOFile f(filename, "rb");
1304 |   bool get_version_header = false;
1305 |   return ReadStateHeaderFromFile(header, f, get_version_header);
1306 | }
1307 | 
1308 | bool ReadWiiMixHeader(const std::string& filename, StateHeader& header)
1309 | {
1310 |   // ensure that the savestate write thread isn't moving around states while we do this
1311 |   std::lock_guard lk(s_save_thread_mutex);
1312 | 
1313 |   File::IOFile f(filename, "rb");
1314 |   bool get_version_header = false;
1315 |   return ReadWiiMixStateHeaderFromFile(header, f, get_version_header);
1316 | }
1317 | 
1318 | std::string GetInfoStringOfSlot(int slot, bool translate)
1319 | {
1320 |   std::string filename = MakeStateFilename(slot);
1321 |   if (!File::Exists(filename))
1322 |     return translate ? Common::GetStringT("Empty") : "Empty";
1323 | 
1324 |   State::StateHeader header;
1325 |   if (!ReadHeader(filename, header))
1326 |     return translate ? Common::GetStringT("Unknown") : "Unknown";
1327 | 
1328 |   return SystemTimeAsDoubleToString(header.legacy_header.time);
1329 | }
1330 | 
1331 | u64 GetUnixTimeOfSlot(int slot)
1332 | {
1333 |   State::StateHeader header;
1334 |   if (!ReadHeader(MakeStateFilename(slot), header))
1335 |     return 0;
1336 | 
1337 |   constexpr u64 MS_PER_SEC = 1000;
1338 |   return static_cast<u64>(header.legacy_header.time * MS_PER_SEC) +
1339 |          (DOUBLE_TIME_OFFSET * MS_PER_SEC);
1340 | }
1341 | 
1342 | static bool DecompressLZ4(std::vector<u8>& raw_buffer, u64 size, File::IOFile& f)
1343 | {
1344 |   raw_buffer.resize(size);
1345 | 
1346 |   u64 total_bytes_read = 0;
1347 |   while (true)
1348 |   {
1349 |     s32 compressed_data_len;
1350 |     if (!f.ReadArray(&compressed_data_len, 1))
1351 |     {
1352 |       PanicAlertFmt("Could not read state data length");
1353 |       return false;
1354 |     }
1355 | 
1356 |     if (compressed_data_len <= 0)
1357 |     {
1358 |       PanicAlertFmtT("Internal LZ4 Error - Tried decompressing {0} bytes", compressed_data_len);
1359 |       return false;
1360 |     }
1361 | 
1362 |     auto compressed_data = std::make_unique<char[]>(compressed_data_len);
1363 |     if (!f.ReadBytes(compressed_data.get(), compressed_data_len))
1364 |     {
1365 |       PanicAlertFmt("Could not read state data");
1366 |       return false;
1367 |     }
1368 | 
1369 |     u32 max_decompress_size =
1370 |         static_cast<u32>(std::min((u64)LZ4_MAX_INPUT_SIZE, size - total_bytes_read));
1371 | 
1372 |     int bytes_read = LZ4_decompress_safe(
1373 |         compressed_data.get(), reinterpret_cast<char*>(raw_buffer.data()) + total_bytes_read,
1374 |         compressed_data_len, max_decompress_size);
1375 | 
1376 |     if (bytes_read < 0)
1377 |     {
1378 |       PanicAlertFmtT("Internal LZ4 Error - decompression failed ({0}, {1}, {2})", bytes_read,
1379 |                      compressed_data_len, max_decompress_size);
1380 |       return false;
1381 |     }
1382 | 
1383 |     total_bytes_read += static_cast<u64>(bytes_read);
1384 | 
1385 |     if (total_bytes_read == size)
1386 |     {
1387 |       return true;
1388 |     }
1389 |     else if (total_bytes_read > size)
1390 |     {
1391 |       PanicAlertFmtT("Internal LZ4 Error - payload size mismatch ({0} / {1}))", total_bytes_read,
1392 |                      size);
1393 |       return false;
1394 |     }
1395 |   }
1396 | }
1397 | 
1398 | static bool ValidateHeaders(const StateHeader& header)
1399 | {
1400 |   bool success = true;
1401 | 
1402 |   // Game ID
1403 |   if (strncmp(SConfig::GetInstance().GetGameID().c_str(), header.legacy_header.game_id, 6))
1404 |   {
1405 |     Core::DisplayMessage(fmt::format("State belongs to a different game (ID {})",
1406 |                                      std::string_view{header.legacy_header.game_id,
1407 |                                                       std::size(header.legacy_header.game_id)}),
1408 |                          2000);
1409 |     return false;
1410 |   }
1411 | 
1412 |   // Check both the state version and the revision string
1413 |   std::string current_str = Common::GetScmRevStr();
1414 |   std::string loaded_str = header.version_string;
1415 |   const u32 loaded_version = header.version_header.version_cookie - COOKIE_BASE;
1416 | 
1417 |   if (s_old_versions.contains(loaded_version))
1418 |   {
1419 |     // This is a REALLY old version, before we started writing the version string to file
1420 |     success = false;
1421 | 
1422 |     std::pair<std::string, std::string> version_range = s_old_versions.find(loaded_version)->second;
1423 |     std::string oldest_version = version_range.first;
1424 |     std::string newest_version = version_range.second;
1425 | 
1426 |     loaded_str = "Dolphin " + oldest_version + " - " + newest_version;
1427 |   }
1428 |   else if (loaded_version != STATE_VERSION)
1429 |   {
1430 |     success = false;
1431 |   }
1432 | 
1433 |   if (!success)
1434 |   {
1435 |     const std::string message =
1436 |         loaded_str.empty() ?
1437 |             "This savestate was created using an incompatible version of Dolphin" :
1438 |             "This savestate was created using the incompatible version " + loaded_str;
1439 |     Core::DisplayMessage(message, OSD::Duration::NORMAL);
1440 |   }
1441 | 
1442 |   return success;
1443 | }
1444 |

1447 |   bool success = true;
1448 | 
1449 |   // Game ID
1450 |   if (strncmp(SConfig::GetInstance().GetGameID().c_str(), header.legacy_header.game_id, 6))
1451 |   {
1452 |     Core::DisplayMessage(fmt::format("State belongs to a different game (ID {})",
1453 |                                      std::string_view{header.legacy_header.game_id,
1454 |                                                       std::size(header.legacy_header.game_id)}),
1455 |                          2000);
1456 |     return false;
1457 |   }
1458 | 
1459 |   // Check both the state version and the revision string
1460 |   std::string current_str = Common::GetScmRevStr();
1461 |   std::string loaded_str = header.version_string;
1462 |   const u32 loaded_version = header.version_header.version_cookie - WIIMIX_COOKIE_BASE;
1463 |

1465 |   {
1466 |     // This is a REALLY old version, before we started writing the version string to file

1470 |     std::string oldest_version = version_range.first;
1471 |     std::string newest_version = version_range.second;
1472 |

1476 |   {

1481 |   {

1491 | 
1492 | static void LoadFileStateData(const std::string& filename, std::vector<u8>& ret_data)
1493 | {
1494 |   File::IOFile f;
1495 | 
1496 |   {
1497 |     // If a state is currently saving, wait for that to end or time out.
1498 |     std::unique_lock lk(s_state_writes_in_queue_mutex);
1499 |     if (s_state_writes_in_queue != 0)
1500 |     {
1501 |       if (!s_state_write_queue_is_empty.wait_for(lk, std::chrono::seconds(3),
1502 |                                                  []() { return s_state_writes_in_queue == 0; }))
1503 |       {
1504 |         Core::DisplayMessage(
1505 |             "A previous state saving operation is still in progress, cancelling load.", 2000);
1506 |         return;
1507 |       }
1508 |     }
1509 |     f.Open(filename, "rb");
1510 |   }
1511 | 
1512 |   StateHeader header;
1513 |   if (!ReadStateHeaderFromFile(header, f) || !ValidateHeaders(header))
1514 |     return;
1515 | 
1516 |   StateExtendedHeader extended_header;
1517 |   if (!f.ReadArray(&extended_header.base_header, 1))
1518 |   {
1519 |     PanicAlertFmt("Unable to read state header");
1520 |     return;
1521 |   }
1522 |   // If StateExtendedHeader is amended to include more than the base, add ReadBytes() calls here.
1523 | 
1524 |   if (extended_header.base_header.header_version != EXTENDED_HEADER_VERSION)
1525 |   {
1526 |     PanicAlertFmt("State header corrupted");
1527 |     return;
1528 |   }
1529 | 
1530 |   std::vector<u8> buffer;
1531 | 
1532 |   switch (extended_header.base_header.compression_type)
1533 |   {
1534 |   case CompressionType::LZ4:
1535 |   {
1536 |     Core::DisplayMessage("Decompressing State...", 500);
1537 |     if (!DecompressLZ4(buffer, extended_header.base_header.uncompressed_size, f))
1538 |       return;
1539 | 
1540 |     break;
1541 |   }
1542 |   case CompressionType::Uncompressed:
1543 |   {
1544 |     u64 header_len = sizeof(StateHeaderLegacy) + sizeof(StateHeaderVersion) +
1545 |                      header.version_header.version_string_length + sizeof(StateExtendedBaseHeader) +
1546 |                      extended_header.base_header.payload_offset;
1547 | 
1548 |     u64 file_size = f.GetSize();
1549 |     if (file_size < header_len)
1550 |     {
1551 |       PanicAlertFmt("State header length corrupted");
1552 |       return;
1553 |     }
1554 | 
1555 |     const auto size = static_cast<size_t>(file_size - header_len);
1556 |     buffer.resize(size);
1557 | 
1558 |     if (!f.ReadBytes(buffer.data(), size))
1559 |     {
1560 |       PanicAlertFmt("Error reading bytes: {0}", size);
1561 |       return;
1562 |     }
1563 |     break;
1564 |   }
1565 |   default:
1566 |     PanicAlertFmt("Unknown compression type {0}", extended_header.base_header.compression_type);
1567 |     return;
1568 |   }
1569 | 
1570 |   // all good
1571 |   ret_data.swap(buffer);
1572 | }
1573 | 
1574 | static void LoadWiiMixFileStateData(const std::string& filename, std::vector<u8>& ret_data)
1575 | {
1576 |   File::IOFile f;
1577 | 
1578 |   {
1579 |     // If a state is currently saving, wait for that to end or time out.
1580 |     std::unique_lock lk(s_state_writes_in_queue_mutex);
1581 |     if (s_state_writes_in_queue != 0)
1582 |     {
1583 |       if (!s_state_write_queue_is_empty.wait_for(lk, std::chrono::seconds(3),
1584 |                                                  []() { return s_state_writes_in_queue == 0; }))
1585 |       {
1586 |         Core::DisplayMessage(
1587 |             "A previous state saving operation is still in progress, cancelling load.", 2000);
1588 |         return;
1589 |       }
1590 |     }
1591 |     f.Open(filename, "rb");
1592 |   }
1593 | 
1594 |   StateHeader header;
1595 |   if (!ReadWiiMixStateHeaderFromFile(header, f) || !ValidateWiiMixHeaders(header))
1596 |     return;
1597 | 
1598 |   StateExtendedHeader extended_header;
1599 |   if (!f.ReadArray(&extended_header.base_header, 1))
1600 |   {
1601 |     PanicAlertFmt("Unable to read state header");
1602 |     return;
1603 |   }
1604 |   // If StateExtendedHeader is amended to include more than the base, add ReadBytes() calls here.
1605 | 
1606 |   if (extended_header.base_header.header_version != EXTENDED_HEADER_VERSION)
1607 |   {
1608 |     PanicAlertFmt("State header corrupted");
1609 |     return;
1610 |   }
1611 | 
1612 |   std::vector<u8> buffer;
1613 | 
1614 |   switch (extended_header.base_header.compression_type)
1615 |   {
1616 |   case CompressionType::LZ4:
1617 |   {
1618 |     Core::DisplayMessage("Decompressing State...", 500);
1619 |     if (!DecompressLZ4(buffer, extended_header.base_header.uncompressed_size, f))
1620 |       return;
1621 | 
1622 |     break;
1623 |   }
1624 |   case CompressionType::Uncompressed:
1625 |   {
1626 |     u64 header_len = sizeof(StateHeaderLegacy) + sizeof(StateHeaderVersion) +
1627 |                      header.version_header.version_string_length + sizeof(StateExtendedBaseHeader) +
1628 |                      extended_header.base_header.payload_offset;
1629 | 
1630 |     u64 file_size = f.GetSize();
1631 |     if (file_size < header_len)
1632 |     {
1633 |       PanicAlertFmt("State header length corrupted");
1634 |       return;
1635 |     }
1636 | 
1637 |     const auto size = static_cast<size_t>(file_size - header_len);
1638 |     buffer.resize(size);
1639 | 
1640 |     if (!f.ReadBytes(buffer.data(), size))
1641 |     {
1642 |       PanicAlertFmt("Error reading bytes: {0}", size);
1643 |       return;
1644 |     }
1645 |     break;
1646 |   }
1647 |   default:
1648 |     PanicAlertFmt("Unknown compression type {0}", extended_header.base_header.compression_type);
1649 |     return;
1650 |   }
1651 | 
1652 |   // all good
1653 |   ret_data.swap(buffer);
1654 | }

1657 | {

1659 | }

1661 | // TODOx
1662 | // Reinitializes host components
1663 | // Minimum Viable Save State
1664 | // - Header
1665 | // - PowerPC::DoState
1666 | // - Memory::DoState
1667 | // - CoreTiming::DoState
1668 | bool WiiMixHostReinitialization(Core::System& system) {
1669 |   if (WIIMIX_JITTER)
1670 |     system.JitterHostEnvironment();
1671 | 
1672 |   system.GetCoreTiming().LogTimingState("Before Host Reinit");
1673 |   system.GetCoreTiming().WiiMixReset();
1674 |   system.GetCoreTiming().LogTimingState("After Host Reinit");
1675 | 
1676 |   // 1. Stop host threads
1677 |   system.GetDSP().Shutdown();
1678 |   system.GetDVDInterface().Shutdown();
1679 | 
1680 |   // FIX: DO NOT SHUTDOWN SOUND STREAM.
1681 |   // Killing the host audio backend causes deadlocks on Linux/PulseAudio during rapid testing.
1682 |   // AudioCommon::WiiMixShutdownSoundStream(system);
1683 | 
1684 |   system.GetPowerPC().Shutdown();
1685 | 
1686 |   // 2. Register All Event Handlers AND Re-init Hardware
1687 |   system.GetSystemTimers().WiiMixReset();
1688 |   system.GetProcessorInterface().WiiMixReset();
1689 |   system.GetMemoryInterface().WiiMixReset();
1690 |   system.GetSerialInterface().WiiMixReset();
1691 |   system.GetGPFifo().WiiMixReset();
1692 |   system.GetHSP().WiiMixReset();
1693 |   system.GetVideoInterface().Init();
1694 |   g_video_backend->WiiMixReset();
1695 | 
1696 |   // 3. Reset Host-Side Configuration
1697 |   system.GetExpansionInterface().WiiMixReset();
1698 |   system.GetMemory().WiiMixReset();
1699 | 
1700 |   // 4. FULLY Re-initialize threaded components
1701 |   system.GetDSP().WiiMixReset();
1702 | 
1703 |   // FIX: DO NOT RESET AUDIO BACKEND HERE
1704 |   system.GetAudioInterface().WiiMixReset();
1705 | 
1706 |   system.GetDVDInterface().WiiMixReset();
1707 | 
1708 |   // 5. Reset Host-Side Caches (CPU JIT)
1709 |   auto& power_pc = system.GetPowerPC();
1710 |   power_pc.Init(State::WIIMIX_DIFF_TEST_CPU_CORE);
1711 | 
1712 |   if (system.IsWii()) {
1713 |     Wiimote::ResetAllWiimotes();
1714 |   }
1715 | 
1716 |   return true;
1717 | }
1718 | 
1719 | void LoadAs(Core::System& system, const std::string& filename)
1720 | {
1721 |   if (!Core::IsRunningOrStarting(system))
1722 |     return;
1723 | 
1724 |   if (NetPlay::IsNetPlayRunning())
1725 |   {
1726 |     OSD::AddMessage("Loading savestates is disabled in Netplay to prevent desyncs");
1727 |     return;
1728 |   }
1729 | 
1730 |   if (AchievementManager::GetInstance().IsHardcoreModeActive())
1731 |   {
1732 |     OSD::AddMessage("Loading savestates is disabled in RetroAchievements hardcore mode");
1733 |     return;
1734 |   }
1735 | 
1736 |   std::unique_lock lk(s_load_or_save_in_progress_mutex, std::try_to_lock);
1737 |   if (!lk)
1738 |     return;
1739 | 
1740 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(
1741 |       system,
1742 |       [&] {
1743 |         // Save temp buffer for undo load state
1744 |         auto& movie = system.GetMovie();
1745 |         if (!movie.IsJustStartingRecordingInputFromSaveState())
1746 |         {
1747 |           std::lock_guard lk2(s_undo_load_buffer_mutex);
1748 |           SaveToBuffer(system, s_undo_load_buffer);
1749 |           const std::string dtmpath = File::GetUserPath(D_STATESAVES_IDX) + "undo.dtm";
1750 |           if (movie.IsMovieActive())
1751 |             movie.SaveRecording(dtmpath);
1752 |           else if (File::Exists(dtmpath))
1753 |             File::Delete(dtmpath);
1754 |         }
1755 | 
1756 |         bool loaded = false;
1757 |         bool loadedSuccessfully = false;
1758 | 
1759 |         // brackets here are so buffer gets freed ASAP
1760 |         {
1761 |           std::vector<u8> buffer;
1762 |           LoadFileStateData(filename, buffer);
1763 | 
1764 |           if (!buffer.empty())
1765 |           {
1766 |             u8* ptr = buffer.data();
1767 |             PointerWrap p(&ptr, buffer.size(), PointerWrap::Mode::Read);
1768 |             DoState(system, p);
1769 |             loaded = true;
1770 |             loadedSuccessfully = p.IsReadMode();
1771 |           }
1772 |         }
1773 | 
1774 |         if (loaded)
1775 |         {
1776 |           if (loadedSuccessfully)
1777 |           {
1778 |             std::filesystem::path tempfilename(filename);
1779 |             Core::DisplayMessage(
1780 |                 fmt::format("Loaded State from {}", tempfilename.filename().string()), 2000);
1781 |             if (File::Exists(filename + ".dtm"))
1782 |               movie.LoadInput(filename + ".dtm");
1783 |             else if (!movie.IsJustStartingRecordingInputFromSaveState() &&
1784 |                      !movie.IsJustStartingPlayingInputFromSaveState())
1785 |               movie.EndPlayInput(false);
1786 |           }
1787 |           else
1788 |           {
1789 |             Core::DisplayMessage("The savestate could not be loaded", OSD::Duration::NORMAL);
1790 | 
1791 |             // since we could be in an inconsistent state now (and might crash or whatever), undo.
1792 |             UndoLoadState(system);
1793 |           }
1794 |         }
1795 | 
1796 |         if (s_on_after_load_callback)
1797 |           s_on_after_load_callback();
1798 |       },
1799 |       true);
1800 | }
1801 | 
1802 | void LoadAsWiiMix(Core::System& system, const std::string& filename)
1803 | {
1804 |   if (!Core::IsRunningOrStarting(system))
1805 |     return;
1806 | 
1807 |   if (NetPlay::IsNetPlayRunning())
1808 |   {
1809 |     OSD::AddMessage("Loading savestates is disabled in Netplay to prevent desyncs");
1810 |     return;
1811 |   }
1812 | 
1813 |   if (AchievementManager::GetInstance().IsHardcoreModeActive())
1814 |   {
1815 |     OSD::AddMessage("Loading savestates is disabled in RetroAchievements hardcore mode");
1816 |     return;
1817 |   }
1818 | 
1819 |   std::unique_lock lk(s_load_or_save_in_progress_mutex, std::try_to_lock);
1820 |   if (!lk)
1821 |     return;
1822 | 
1823 |   Core::RunOnCPUThread(
1824 |       system,
1825 |       [&] {
1826 |         // Save temp buffer for undo load state
1827 |         auto& movie = system.GetMovie();
1828 |         if (!movie.IsJustStartingRecordingInputFromSaveState())
1829 |         {
1830 |           std::lock_guard lk2(s_undo_load_buffer_mutex);
1831 |           WiiMixSaveToBuffer(system, s_undo_load_buffer);
1832 |           const std::string dtmpath = File::GetUserPath(D_STATESAVES_IDX) + "undo.dtm";
1833 |           if (movie.IsMovieActive())
1834 |             movie.SaveRecording(dtmpath);
1835 |           else if (File::Exists(dtmpath))
1836 |             File::Delete(dtmpath);
1837 |         }
1838 | 
1839 |         bool loaded = false;
1840 |         bool loadedSuccessfully = false;
1841 | 
1842 |         // brackets here are so buffer gets freed ASAP
1843 |         {
1844 |           std::vector<u8> buffer;
1845 |           LoadWiiMixFileStateData(filename, buffer);
1846 | 
1847 |           if (!buffer.empty())
1848 |           {
1849 |             loadedSuccessfully = WiiMixLoadFromBuffer(system, buffer);
1850 |             loaded = true;
1851 |           }
1852 |         }
1853 | 
1854 |         if (loaded)
1855 |         {
1856 |           if (loadedSuccessfully)
1857 |           {
1858 |             std::filesystem::path tempfilename(filename);
1859 |             Core::DisplayMessage(
1860 |                 fmt::format("Loaded State from {}", tempfilename.filename().string()), 2000);
1861 |             if (File::Exists(filename + ".dtm"))
1862 |               movie.LoadInput(filename + ".dtm");
1863 |             else if (!movie.IsJustStartingRecordingInputFromSaveState() &&
1864 |                      !movie.IsJustStartingPlayingInputFromSaveState())
1865 |               movie.EndPlayInput(false);
1866 |           }
1867 |           else
1868 |           {
1869 |             Core::DisplayMessage("The savestate could not be loaded", OSD::Duration::NORMAL);
1870 | 
1871 |             // since we could be in an inconsistent state now (and might crash or whatever), undo.
1872 |             UndoWiiMixLoadState(system);
1873 |           }
1874 |         }
1875 | 
1876 |         if (s_on_after_load_callback)
1877 |           s_on_after_load_callback();
1878 |       },
1879 |       true);
1880 | }

1886 | 
1887 | void Init(Core::System& system)
1888 | {
1889 |   s_save_thread.Reset("Savestate Worker", [&system](CompressAndDumpState_args args) {
1890 |     CompressAndDumpState(system, args);
1891 | 
1892 |     {
1893 |       std::lock_guard lk(s_state_writes_in_queue_mutex);
1894 |       if (--s_state_writes_in_queue == 0)
1895 |         s_state_write_queue_is_empty.notify_all();
1896 |     }
1897 | 
1898 |     if (args.state_write_done_event)
1899 |       args.state_write_done_event->Set();
1900 |   });
1901 | 
1902 |   s_wiimix_save_thread.Reset("WiiMix Savestate Worker", [&system](CompressAndDumpState_args args) {
1903 |     // Tell it to call your new dump function
1904 |     CompressAndDumpWiiMixState(system, args);
1905 | 
1906 |     {
1907 |       std::lock_guard lk(s_state_writes_in_queue_mutex);
1908 |       if (--s_state_writes_in_queue == 0)
1909 |         s_state_write_queue_is_empty.notify_all();
1910 |     }
1911 | 
1912 |     if (args.state_write_done_event)
1913 |       args.state_write_done_event->Set();
1914 |   });
1915 | }
1916 | 
1917 | void Shutdown()
1918 | {
1919 |   s_save_thread.Shutdown();
1920 |   s_wiimix_save_thread.Shutdown();
1921 | 
1922 |   // swapping with an empty vector, rather than clear()ing
1923 |   // this gives a better guarantee to free the allocated memory right NOW (as opposed to, actually,
1924 |   // never)
1925 |   {
1926 |     std::lock_guard lk(s_undo_load_buffer_mutex);
1927 |     std::vector<u8>().swap(s_undo_load_buffer);
1928 |   }
1929 | }
